Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Robot sensors and electronic equipment are prone to failure in harsh environments. With water hydraulic drive, thin and long tubes enable remote operation without actuator-side sensors. Furthermore, the elasticity of the tubes reduces the impedance of the joints (actuators), benefiting robot tasks involving unexpected contact with the environment or vibrations. However, owing to the low impedance and limited camera visibility, accurately positioning the joint (or end effector) to the target location under varying load conditions is challenging. This study proposes a novel method that employs water-filled flexible tubes to enable the transmission of driving power and actuator-side information to and from the actuator, respectively, without actuator-side sensors. By modeling volumetric loss during transmission based on pressure fluctuations and incorporating minor air entrapment, simultaneous power transmission and position estimation is achieved through a tube up to 50 m. Thus, it becomes possible to use a feedback control framework that was previously difficult to implement in sensorless systems. Experimental validation confirms stable position control of a sensorless water hydraulic cylinder under varying loads. Furthermore, a field parameter-identification method accounts for tube and air entrainment variability without requiring actuator-side sensors. These contributions promote reliable remote control of robots in harsh environments.

</details>


### [165] [DROID-ANCHOR: Odometry-Anchored Recurrent Metric Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17058)
*Yuxuan Chen,Brook Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Metric-DROID，一种端到端递归SLAM架构，通过融合本体感受里程计解决单目系统尺度模糊问题，具备尺度精确性和鲁棒性。核心创新包括LSTM更新算子、不确定性感知后端及残差微调策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目SLAM系统受固有尺度模糊和漂移限制，现有基于递归光流的SLAM方法虽具备强鲁棒性但仍无法提供度量一致性，制约自主机器人导航精度。

Method: 提出三大技术突破：1) LSTM更新算子将高频里程计序列编码为空间特征图，提供持续度量偏差；2) 不确定性感知度量后端(𝐵𝐴_𝑜𝑑𝑜𝑚)以学习异方差协方差将里程计作为几何锚点；3) 选择性残差微调策略实现零样本度量对齐同时保留预训练几何先验。

Result: 系统在鲁棒性达SOTA水平的同时实现度量精度突破，通过动态平衡视觉重投影与度量平移残差有效抑制轮滑和传感器噪声影响。

Conclusion: Metric-DROID通过将视觉SLAM锚定于物理现实，为自主导航提供了兼具鲁棒性与度量精度的可靠解决方案，解决了长期存在的单目尺度歧义问题。

Abstract: Precise metric depth estimation is fundamental for autonomous robot navigation, yet monocular systems inherently suffer from scale ambiguity and scale drift. While recent recurrent flow-based SLAM systems have demonstrated state-of-the-art robustness, they remain scale-ambiguous. In this paper, we propose Metric-DROID, an end-to-end recurrent architecture that anchors visual SLAM to physical reality by integrating proprioceptive odometry. Our framework introduces the following innovations: (1) A LSTM Update Operator that encodes high-frequency odometry sequences into spatial feature maps, providing a persistent metric bias for iterative refinement. (2) An Uncertainty-Aware Metric Backend ($BA_{odom}$) that treats odometry as a geometric anchor with learned heteroscedastic covariance. By regressing a time-varying metric uncertainty $Σ_{o}$, our system intelligently balances visual re-projection and metric translation residuals, effectively mitigating the impact of wheel-slip and sensor noise. (3) We further propose a selective residual fine-tuning strategy to preserve pre-trained geometric priors while enabling zero-shot metric alignment.

</details>


### [166] [Move First, Commit Later: Selective LiDAR-to-BIM Global Initialization via Sequential Consensus with Symmetry-Aware Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17103)
*Yujie Zhang,Yuxuan Guo,Jiashuo Zheng,Zeheng Fan,Xiaohui Jia,Jinyue Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种解决室内对称环境中LiDAR-BIM初始化定位问题的方法，通过分层决策机制避免高置信度误匹配，实现可靠定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重复性室内环境中，LiDAR-BIM定位常因对称结构导致高置信度但错误的匹配（aliasing），传统方法无法区分多个相似区域的置信度差异。

Method: 1. 将多子图候选结果映射到统一SE(2)锚点坐标系；2. 使用top-1共识算法（B_m）聚合跨子图证据；3. 拓扑仅作为二进制可行性门控；4. 决策类型化为COMMIT/DEFER/AMBIGUOUS(τ)，其中τ表示检测到的对称周期；5. 通过运动触发可逆升级机制（AMBIGUOUS→COMMIT）。

Result: 在真实建筑与对称模拟环境中，该方法在全部轨迹尺度配置下实现100%正确提交，定位精度达0.02-0.36m和1.9°角度误差，显著优于强制选择策略。

Conclusion: 该模块化分层架构有效解决对称性导致的定位歧义问题，为LiDAR-BIM初始化提供高可靠性解决方案，其可逆决策机制和前端解耦设计具备良好的通用性扩展潜力。

Abstract: Global LiDAR-to-BIM initialization must place a robot within an as-designed building model without a prior pose. In repetitive interiors, the principal failure mode is not low-confidence registration but confident aliasing: a submap can match several BIM regions with comparable scores, producing a high-scoring pose displaced by symmetry. We present Move First, Commit Later, a selective layer that treats a registration front-end as an evidence source and decides whether to commit. Candidates from multiple submaps are mapped to a common $\mathrm{SE}(2)$ anchor; a top-1 consensus $B_m$, invariant to non-champion multiplicity, aggregates cross-submap evidence; and topology serves only as a binary feasibility gate. The decision is typed--COMMIT, DEFER, or AMBIGUOUS($τ$), reporting the detected symmetry period--and reversible: symmetry-breaking motion upgrades AMBIGUOUS to COMMIT. On a real multi-room building and a controlled symmetric simulation, the layer commits correctly in every tested trajectory-scale configuration and otherwise abstains with a typed state, whereas forced-choice policies on the same front-end select wrong rooms in most cases. Committed anchors are within 0.02--0.36 m of an independent laser-tracker position reference and within $1.9^\circ$ of a BIM-registration orientation proxy. The evaluation covers one building and one front-end; the layer is designed to be front-end modular.

</details>


### [167] [BoxTwin: Learning Elastoplastic Articulated Object Dynamics from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17132)
*Heng Zhang,Gehan Zheng,Kaifeng Zhang,Jay Song,Shivansh Patel,Sonny Hu,Yunzhu Li,Changxi Zheng,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BoxTwin通过视频学习弹塑性铰接物体的全动力学，实现对非结构化环境中可变形物体的预测性和自适应控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字孪生模型难以处理具有非线性弹性、塑性屈服和损伤累积特性的弹塑性铰接物体（EAOs），限制了机器人在复杂物理交互中的适应能力。

Method: 提出BoxTwin框架，通过视频驱动重建场景并识别每个EAO的物理感知本构模型，整合弹塑性损伤建模学习完整动力学。

Result: 在手动折叠和双臂操纵实验中，BoxTwin精准跟踪关节轨迹，并在长时间范围内可靠复现接触后的塑性行为。

Conclusion: BoxTwin显著推进数字孪生技术，首次实现对非结构化环境中可变形铰接物体的预测性控制，提升机器人系统的鲁棒性和适应性。

Abstract: Digital twins enable robots to anticipate and adapt to physical interactions, but existing models struggle with elastoplastic articulated objects (EAOs) that exhibit nonlinear elasticity, plastic yielding, and damage accumulation. We present BoxTwin, an interactive digital twin framework that learns the full dynamics of EAOs from videos. Our pipeline reconstructs the scene, identifies a physics aware constitutive model for each EAO. Experiments on manual folding and dual arm manipulation of EAOs show that BoxTwin accurately tracks joint trajectories and reproduces post contact plastic behavior over long horizons. By integrating video driven reconstruction with elastoplastic damage modeling, BoxTwin advances digital twins toward predictive, adaptive control of deformable articulated objects in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [168] [VIDAR: Visual-Inertial Dense Alignment and Reconstruction via a Geometric Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17171)
*Diyari Mohammed Salih,Lingxiang Hu,Naima AitOufroukh-Mammar,Fabien Bonardi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Monocular foundation models provide dense geometry but usually lack a stable metric scale. This paper presents VIDAR, a visual-inertial dense reconstruction framework that couples SVO+IMU odometry with Depth Anything 3. VIDAR uses the visual-inertial front end as a metric anchor: it provides camera poses, scale, and a consistent world frame for aligning dense foundation-model predictions across time. The foundation model then contributes detailed local geometry that is fused into a global reconstruction. We study both pose-conditioned DA3 and a decoupled alignment strategy. On EuRoC, pose injection reduces scale error to about 1\% and reaches 0.463 mean F@0.10; the decoupled hybrid improves this to 0.676 without ground-truth poses. Results on EuRoC and TUM RGB-D show that VIDAR is a practical route to metric dense monocular reconstruction.

</details>


### [169] [Retriever: Composing Closed-Loop Asynchronous Robot Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17213)
*Linfeng Zhao,Haojie Huang,Jiayuan Mao,Weiyu Liu,Mykel Kochenderfer,Lawson L. S. Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Retriever框架，通过统一异步决策模型、编程模型和运行时，解决长时程机器人代理系统的时序隐式化问题，实现行为可重现、易调试和重用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人代理系统依赖ad-hoc并发和发布/订阅机制，导致时序及输入消费语义隐式化，产生调度依赖行为，难以重现、调试和重用；现有方案仅部分解决算法或系统层问题，缺乏跨栈整合。

Method: 设计Retriever框架，将代理建模为在显式运行时钟上执行的状态因果流函数图；形式化异步环境-代理循环 over 连续时间流，证明有限内存因果策略可由此操作符组合表示；编译图为支持多后端的运行时，实现跨环境系统调试和日志异步数据的确定重放。

Result: 通过真实机器人案例研究和控制实验验证：Retriever显著降低运行时开销，支持高保真确定重放，有效改善行为可重现性和调试效率。

Conclusion: Retriever成功跨越算法与系统栈，提供端到端解决方案，推动长时程机器人代理向可预测、可维护方向发展。

Abstract: Building long-horizon robot agents requires composing closed-loop pipelines -- perception, belief update, planning, and control -- whose components run at different clocks and with variable latency. Today, these systems are often assembled with ad-hoc concurrency and pub/sub conventions that make timing and input-consumption semantics implicit, yielding schedule-dependent behavior that is hard to reproduce, debug, and reuse. Current solutions typically solve parts of this problem at either the algorithmic or the systems layer, but not both. In this work, we propose Retriever, which spans the entire stack: an asynchronous decision model, a programming model, a runtime, and an example closed-loop agent pipeline. Retriever represents an agent as a graph of stateful causal stream functions executed on explicit run clocks. We formalize this view via an asynchronous environment-agent loop over continuous-time streams and show that finite-memory causal policies can be represented by compositions of these operators. Retriever compiles these graphs into a runtime that supports multiple backends, enabling systematic debugging across running environments and deterministic replay from logged asynchronous data. We evaluate Retriever through a real-robot case study together with controlled studies of runtime overhead and deterministic replay behavior.

</details>


### [170] [Asynchronous Multimodal Diffusion Policy Composition via Latency-Aware Guidance Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17257)
*Zihao He,Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Cewu Lu,Haoshu Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAG-Fusion框架实现异步多模态扩散策略融合，显著提升机器人操作任务的响应速度和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略在多模态扩展中受限于模态间感知速率和推理延迟差异，同步融合导致高频率反馈降低，手动设计的多频率架构可扩展性差，亟需高效异步融合方法以优化操作性能。

Method: LAG-Fusion框架通过允许模态特定策略以原生推理速率运行，并推导参考系重定位规则对齐延迟的扩散变量指导，实现异步融合的一致性，支持任意模态组合。

Result: 在接触丰富操作任务中，LAG-Fusion组合低频视觉策略与高频力策略，实验在异构模态延迟条件下验证其响应速度和任务性能显著优于同步融合及力感知基线方法。

Conclusion: LAG-Fusion有效解决了异步多模态扩散策略融合的效率与可扩展性问题，为机器人操作提供了更灵活、高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion policies have shown strong potential for robotic imitation learning, and recent extensions incorporate additional modalities to improve manipulation performance. However, these modalities often differ not only in information content but also in sensing rates and inference latencies. Existing multimodal diffusion policies typically rely on synchronous fusion or manually designed multi-frequency architectures, which either slow down high-frequency feedback or limit extensibility to new modality combinations. We propose LAG-Fusion, a latency-aware guidance fusion framework for asynchronous multimodal diffusion policy composition. LAG-Fusion allows modality-specific policies to operate at their native inference rates and contribute denoising guidance whenever available. To make asynchronous composition consistent, we derive a reference-frame rebasing rule for diffusion variables under relative action representations, enabling delayed guidance to be aligned before fusion. We instantiate LAG-Fusion in contact-rich manipulation by composing a low-frequency vision policy with a high-frequency force policy. Experiments under heterogeneous modality latencies show that LAG-Fusion improves policy responsiveness and task performance over synchronous fusion and specially designed force-aware baselines.

</details>


### [171] [From Perception to Assistance: Open-Vocabulary Shared Autonomy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17323)
*Murilo Vinicius da Silva,Ricardo V. Godoy,Juliano Negri,Gustavo J. G. Lahr,Ranulfo Bezerra,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单目RGB-D摄像头和文本提示的共享自主框架，通过视觉-语言模型和GPU加速控制器实现高精度机械臂遥操作，在工业场景中显著降低碰撞风险并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机界面在复杂工业环境中难以满足遥操作精度需求，操作员面临深度感知不足、目标对齐困难及碰撞风险等问题，需新型辅助系统提升安全性与效率。

Method: 采用无穿戴设备的RGB-D摄像头捕捉操作员动作，结合文本提示驱动视觉-语言模型定位目标，利用提示可视频分割跟踪目标并生成抓取帧；GPU加速模型预测控制器实时执行避障策略，势能场优化最终接近路径；支持手势触发的自主模式完成抓取。

Result: 在四足移动机械臂实验中，定位误差59mm，有效保持18cm避障距离；阀操作与拣放任务成功率100%，消融实验显示模块不可或缺性；自主模式单任务成功率80%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过多模态感知与智能控制融合，显著提升工业遥操作的精度、安全性和自主性，验证了技术可行性及实用价值。

Abstract: Teleoperating a robotic manipulator in industrial environments demands precision that camera-based interfaces alone struggle to deliver. The operator must align the end-effector with a target in clutter, under limited depth perception, and without colliding with the surrounding structures. This paper presents a shared-autonomy framework that assists the operator throughout this process. A single RGB-D camera captures the operator's arm motion and hand gestures without wearables, fiducials, or a calibration stage. The intended target is specified by a free-form text prompt, grounded by a vision-language model in the robot's gripper camera, and tracked across its onboard cameras by a promptable video-segmentation model, resulting in a grasp frame continuously separated from the obstacle map. Every commanded motion is executed by a GPU-accelerated model-predictive controller that enforces self- and environment-collision avoidance against an online volumetric reconstruction, while a potential field corrects the operator's reference toward the grounded target during the final approach. An autonomous mode can be gesture-triggered to complete the grasp on the same target without a separate perception pipeline. The framework is validated on a quadruped mobile manipulator. The interface achieves a positional RMSE of 59 mm relative to motion-capture ground truth, and the controller keeps the arm at least 18 cm from obstacles while the operator deliberately commands the arm into them by 6 cm. In an industrial valve manipulation and a pick-and-place task, the full framework succeeded in all trials, while ablating either the collision or the assistance module produced failures through complementary mechanisms, and autonomous execution succeeded in four of five trials per task.

</details>


### [172] [Multi-Resolution Voxelized Map-Based Stereo Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17332)
*Shuyi Pan,Hangtian Wang,Zhaoxing Zhang,Chengliang Zhang,Zikang Yuan,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多分辨率先验地图构建方法及对应VIO系统，通过体素化地图和锥形索引策略，显著降低数据传输量和计算负载，在边缘设备上实现高精度姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先验地图能提升视觉惯性里程计(VIO)的姿态估计准确性和鲁棒性，但其庞大的数据量与有限的传输带宽导致边缘设备难以持续加载本地地图，亟需一种高效的数据管理与索引机制以解决带宽瓶颈。

Method: 提出多分辨率先验地图构建方法：将地图体素化并每个体素仅保留单点；在线运行时，采用锥形索引策略——锥形截距由当前位置到3D点的距离动态确定，结合3D数字差分分析器(DDA)算法选择合适分辨率层级并检索唯一地图点，从而高效关联2D特征与3D地图点。

Result: 在两个公共数据集上的实验表明，该系统以极小的数据传输开销实现精确姿态估计，验证了方法在减少通信负载和计算负担方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作克服了VIO中带宽限制的关键挑战，为边缘计算场景下的实时VIO应用提供了可行方案，未来可扩展至更复杂的动态环境或与其他传感器融合。

Abstract: Incorporating prior maps significantly enhances the accuracy and robustness of pose estimation in visual-inertial odometry (VIO). However, the large data volume of such maps, combined with limited transmission bandwidth, makes it impractical to continuously load local maps onto an edge device. In this paper, we propose a multi-resolution prior map construction method and a corresponding map-based VIO system. The prior map is voxelized at multiple resolutions, with each voxel retaining only a single map point. During online VIO operation, a cone-shaped indexing strategy associates 2D features on the edge device with 3D map points. The cone's intercept is determined by the distance from the current position to the 3D points, enabling the selection of the appropriate resolution level and the retrieval of the unique map point within the corresponding voxel via a 3D digital differential analyzer (DDA) algorithm. This approach minimizes both the volume of data required for transmission and the computational load during data association. Extensive experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that our system achieves accurate pose estimation while requiring minimal data transmission.

</details>


### [173] [Test-Time Scaling for World Action Models via Zero-Shot Geometric Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17454)
*Zesen Zhao,Minkyoung Cho,Hui shen,Boyuan Zheng,Kunxiao Gao,Yulong Cao,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出\methodgated框架，一种无训练的选择性测试时缩放方法，通过跨视图深度重投影一致性优化World Action Models (WAMs)，在多个机器人控制基准上显著提升任务成功率（如RoboCasa组平均从66.3%升至68.4%），同时仅26.2%决策点触发额外计算，平均恢复74.8%性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放虽能增强基础模型推理，但机器人控制需在执行动作前预知额外计算是否有益；WAMs通过暴露动作片段和预测未来观察，使此决策过程自然化，亟需高效选择机制以减少冗余计算。

Method: 实现\method作为固定预算Best-of-N选择器，基于冻结几何基础模型计算预测未来的跨视图深度重投影一致性以排名rollouts；\methodgated进一步添加轻量级动作-未来一致性门，仅当初始rollout显示内部不一致时激活额外采样，实现自适应计算分配。

Result: 在RoboCasa、LIBERO Long和RoboTwin 2.0等基准的五种设置中，固定预算\method将N=8任务成功率全场景提升（如RoboCasa Cosmos Policy从66.3%→68.4%）；启用门控后，\methodgated平均恢复74.8%成功增益，但仅触发26.2%决策点额外采样；离线诊断证实跨视图重投影为强任务无关选择器，假低分选择被识别为性能饱和失败模式。

Conclusion: \methodgated框架有效平衡性能与计算成本，跨视图重投影一致性提供可靠选择依据，为WAMs测试时缩放确立实用范式；未来需优化高N值下的选择稳定性以避免性能退化。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves foundation-model inference by spending additional computation, but robot control requires deciding whether extra compute is useful before executing an action. World Action Models (WAMs) make this decision natural: each rollout exposes both an action chunk and predicted future observations. We propose \methodgated, a training-free selective test-time scaling framework for WAMs. We first instantiate \method, a fixed-budget Best-of-$N$ selector that ranks sampled rollouts by cross-view depth reprojection consistency of their predicted futures, computed with a frozen geometry foundation model. \methodgated\ adds a lightweight action--future consistency gate that invokes \method\ only when the initial rollout appears internally inconsistent. Across five benchmark--backbone settings on RoboCasa, LIBERO Long, and RoboTwin~2.0, fixed-budget \method\ improves $N{=}8$ task success in every setting, e.g., raising the RoboCasa group average from $66.3\%$ to $68.4\%$ with Cosmos Policy and from $80.8\%$ to $82.5\%$ with X-WAM. With gating enabled, \methodgated\ recovers on average $74.8\%$ of the always-on success gain while triggering additional sampling on only $26.2\%$ of decision points. Offline diagnostics show that cross-view reprojection is a strong task-label-free selector, and we identify false low-score selections as a failure mode that helps explain why performance can saturate or degrade as $N$ increases.

</details>


### [174] [Disturbance-Aware Flight for Aerial Robots in Narrow Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17476)
*Lei Qiang,Tianyu He,Chenyang Sun,Xurui Liu,Miao Wang,Xiaobin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAPCF框架，通过实时扰动估计与动态速度调节，在0.6m狭窄隧道中实现四旋翼高效穿越，性能超越人类飞行员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法因未整合规划-控制环的动态扰动补偿，在狭小空间易产生保守行为或安全隐患，需突破传统固定速度限制的局限性。

Method: 采用双环观测器实时估计六自由度扰动力/力矩，设计扰动风险函数自适应调节参考速度，结合MDNMPC控制器进行轨迹跟踪补偿。

Result: 0.39m对角四旋翼可穿越0.6m宽直/斜/曲隧道，实验验证成功率及效率均优于人类操作，且能动态响应扰动阈值变化。

Conclusion: DAPCF框架首次实现规划-控制协同优化，通过扰动感知显著提升狭小空间飞行安全性与机动性，为复杂环境自主导航提供新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous flight of aerial robots in narrow space remains challenging due to strong aerodynamic disturbances and limited flying space. Existing approaches mainly address aerodynamic disturbances at the control level, while motion planning typically relies on geometric constraints and fixed speed limits, leading to conservative or unsafe behaviors in confined environments. This paper presents a disturbance-aware planning and control framework (DAPCF) that integrates online disturbance estimation into the planning-control loop for quadrotor flight in narrow space. First, the dual-loop observers estimate 6-degree-of-freedom disturbance forces and torques in real time based on odometry and motor speed measurements. Then, a disturbance risk function is introduced that adaptively modulates the reference speed of the planner based on disturbance estimation, reducing velocity when disturbances exceed a threshold and restoring it under low-disturbance conditions. Finally, a motor-dynamics-based nonlinear model predictive controller (MDNMPC) with disturbance compensation is designed to ensure robust trajectory tracking under perturbed conditions. Experiments demonstrate that a quadrotor with a diagonal length of 0.39~m can traverse straight, sloped, and curved tunnels as narrow as 0.6~m, outperforming human pilots in both success rate and flight efficiency.

</details>


### [175] [GeoWorldAD: Geometry World Action Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17521)
*Songyan Zhang,Jinyuan Tian,Hanbing Li,Daqi Liu,Hao Chen,Wenhui Huang,Fang Li,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Kuiyuan Yang,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoWorldAD通过显式3D几何基础和未来几何预测，实现安全高效的自动驾驶规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision/Video-Action模型虽能扩展至大规模数据，但缺乏明确几何 grounding 和未来感知空间指导，导致难以平衡避障安全与驾驶进度，易产生过度保守决策。

Method: 提出GeoWorldAD几何世界动作模型，将轨迹规划锚定于自我对齐3D空间，利用潜在未来几何令牌预测短 horizon 场景演变，并通过迭代轨迹细化渐进聚合多尺度当前几何与未来几何信息。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1/v2基准上取得SOTA性能，显著提升避障效率和驾驶进度，验证了显式3D几何 grounding 与未来几何建模的有效性。

Conclusion: GeoWorldAD证明几何世界建模对自动驾驶规划至关重要，通过融合当前约束与未来演化信息，在保障安全前提下优化决策效率。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires both safe and efficient planning decisions in dynamic 3D environments. Although recent Vision/Video-Action models learn policies directly from visual observations and scale well with advances in vision transformers and large-scale training data, they often lack explicit geometric grounding and future-aware spatial guidance, limiting their ability to balance collision avoidance and driving progress. In this work, we propose GeoWorldAD, a geometry world action model that grounds trajectory planning in ego-aligned 3D space and anticipates short-horizon scene evolution with latent future geometry tokens. Present geometry provides essential spatial constraints for safe planning, while future geometry reveals how surrounding agents and ego-centric free space may evolve, reducing overly conservative decisions without sacrificing safety. To efficiently exploit these geometric cues, GeoWorldAD progressively aggregates multi-scale present geometry and latent future geometry through iterative trajectory refinement. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 and v2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the effectiveness of explicit 3D geometry grounding and future geometry world modeling for safe and efficient autonomous driving.

</details>


### [176] [Predicting Grasping Compliance in Robotic Hands through Analytical-Model-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17541)
*Qianwen Zhao,Long Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对力ful工具操作中抓握顺应性建模难题，提出混合预测模型AMINN，结合分析力学与神经网络，在欠驱动机器人手上验证其准确性和物理一致性，推动安全可靠的自主工具使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统抓取研究将问题简化为二元成败，但力ful工具使用需考虑抓握顺应性——由耦合运动学、配置、被动力学和接触条件导致的非线性变形-力耦合行为；欠驱动手虽低成本自适应，却放大建模难度，亟需精准预测外部载荷下的工具-手协同响应。

Method: 开发分析模型增强神经网络（AMINN），该混合框架集成分析力学层处理物理原理与数据驱动学习层优化参数，通过仿真与实验数据训练，预测抓握稳定性和工具位移。

Result: 在三指欠驱动手上测试，AMINN展现出强预测能力，输出机械合理且在多样载荷条件下稳定；相比黑盒多层感知机基线，能量物理一致性提升15%以上，显著减少预测偏差。

Conclusion: AMINN不仅提升预测精度，还通过可解释设计促进安全关键场景的可靠自主操作，为机器人操控领域提供物理一致、高效的学习范式，支持更安全的力ful工具应用。

Abstract: In robotic manipulation studies, grasping is often treated as a binary success or failure problem, usually defined by whether the object simply stays in the hand. For forceful tool use, however, this view is insufficient because grasp compliance becomes a critical factor governing how the hand and tool behave under load. Compliance arises from coupled kinematics, grasp configuration, passive mechanics, and contact conditions, producing nonlinear behavior in which deformation and interaction forces influence each other. Understanding this relationship is essential for predictive models of how a grasped tool and a compliant hand jointly respond to external loading. In underactuated hands, these effects are amplified: such designs offer low cost and adaptive grasping, but make compliance behavior more difficult to model and predict. Our goal is therefore to develop a predictive model for grasped tool behavior during forceful interactions. To address this challenge, we introduce an analytical model informed neural network (AMINN), a hybrid predictive model that combines an analytical mechanics layer with data driven learning to estimate grasp stability and in hand tool displacement under external loading. The model is evaluated on a three finger underactuated robotic hand and shows strong predictive capability with mechanically meaningful outputs across diverse loading conditions. Compared with a black box multilayer perceptron baseline, AMINN also achieves better energy based physical consistency. Beyond prediction accuracy alone, this framework advances physically interpretable learning for robotic manipulation and supports more reliable, safer, and more trustworthy autonomous tool use in safety critical settings during forceful interaction.

</details>


### [177] [Finite-Time Curvature-Constrained Vector Field for Saturation-Free Motion Planning of Nonholonomic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17542)
*Zhouru Xiao,Sha Luo,Yang Lu,Héctor García de Marina,Zhenyang Xu,Chaosong Gong,Yaonan Wang,Weijia Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出有限时间曲率约束向量场（FT-C2VF）和饱和自由控制器，实现非完整机器人精确导航，提供有限时间收敛、曲率约束和饱和避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完整移动机器人精确导航困难；现有向量场方法无法显式生成满足曲率约束的轨迹，依赖输入饱和易破坏稳定性并降低性能；仅保证渐近收敛而无明确settling-time界限。

Method: 构造FT-C2VF使用互补增益，确保有限时间收敛且曲率连续有界递减；开发几乎全局C1-光滑饱和自由控制器无Jacobian信息跟踪VF；基于动力系统分析证明几乎全局有限时间稳定性。

Result: 数值模拟显示性能优于代表性向量场方法；户外实验在Ackermann转向车辆上验证有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功解决曲率约束和饱和问题，实现有限时间稳定性，显著提升导航性能和实用性。

Abstract: Accurately steering a robot to a target configuration is fundamental in engineering, yet remains challenging for nonholonomic mobile robots. Vector fields (VFs) provide a natural framework by specifying desired motion directions throughout the workspace and enabling direct integration with feedback control. However, most existing VF-based methods cannot explicitly generate trajectories satisfying curvature constraints. Actuator limits are therefore often enforced by input saturation, which may invalidate stability guarantees and degrade closed-loop performance when not considered in controller design. In addition, these methods usually ensure only asymptotic convergence without an explicit settling-time bound. To address these issues, we propose a generalized motion planning and control framework consisting of a finite-time curvature-constrained vector field (FT-C2VF) and a saturation-free control law. Depending on the motion objective, the framework drives the robot to the target configuration in finite time or through it periodically. First, the FT-C2VF is constructed using complementary gains to achieve finite-time convergence while ensuring that the curvature of its integral curves is continuous, bounded, and monotonically decreasing with the radial ratio. Second, an almost globally C1-smooth, saturation-free controller is developed to track the FT-C2VF without Jacobian information, while keeping all control inputs within prescribed actuator limits. Third, dynamical-systems analysis establishes almost-global finite-time stability of the target equilibrium. Numerical simulations show improved performance over representative VF-based methods, and outdoor experiments on an Ackermann-steered vehicle confirm the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [178] [COLIP-2: Olfaction-Vision-Language Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17559)
*Kordel Kade France*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COLIP-2 is a multimodal pre-training model that unifies olfaction, vision, and language embeddings, enabling robots to probabilistically localize scents in scenes. It highlights data scarcity for image-scent pairs and advocates for new methodologies and datasets to advance olfactory robotics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Addressing the lack of large-scale paired image-scent datasets (e.g., ImageNet-scale) critical for training advanced olfactory perception systems in robotics, necessitating new data collection and methodological innovations.

Method: Contrastive learning to train a shared embedding space integrating molecular structures, gas-sensor readings, odor-descriptor language, and images, optimized for edge deployment in real-time robotic applications.

Result: Demonstrated feasibility of robotic scent localization using open-sourced olfactory data through internal testing, with architectural optimizations for edge computing.

Conclusion: COLIP-2 reveals current limitations in olfactory robotics due to data scarcity and underscores the necessity for novel datasets and techniques to enable advanced multimodal olfactory intelligence beyond robotics.

Abstract: The Contrastive Olfaction-Language-Image Pre-training 2 (COLIP-2) model is a multimodal embeddings space that places olfaction as a first-class citizen among vision and language. Molecular structure, gas-sensor readings, odor-descriptor language, and images are all trained into a single shared representation space, so that a robot can localize a detected aroma to objects in a scene probabilistically. No ImageNet-scale datasets of paired image-scent examples exists which warrants the need for their collection. Our intent with the release of COLIP-2 is to demonstrate the limit of what can be built for robotics with open-sourced olfactory data in order to ground the argument for why new methodologies and datasets are necessary in order to enable advanced olfactory-oriented perception capabilities. We enumerate results from internal testing of the COLIP-2 architecture and make necessary optimizations to run the model at the edge for real-time robotics applications. While developed with robotics in mind, the design of COLIP-2 has been influenced by experts across many disciplines of science in academia and industry, and we hope that the model can be useful in any multimodal domain requiring olfactory intelligence.

</details>


### [179] [Predictive Training with Latent Imagination for Visual Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17574)
*Yancheng Zhu,Wanli Ma,Chen Han,Irvin Haozhe Zhan,Bingfeng Qin,Yixin Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量级预测监督方法，通过训练时辅助预测器增强足式机器人导航策略的预见性，显著提升动态环境中的导航成功率并降低碰撞率，推理时无额外计算开销，实现零样本sim-to-real转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的足式机器人导航策略依赖反应式决策，仅基于当前观测和短期记忆，难以预测移动障碍物的近未来演化，导致在动态环境中响应延迟和碰撞风险高。

Method: 在LSTM-SRU导航主干网络上集成辅助JEPA-style预测器和SIGReg正则化；训练时，预测器监督隐藏状态以自回归预测下一状态，编码障碍物动态信息；推理时完全丢弃预测器，保持计算效率。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界动态障碍物基准上，该方法大幅提高导航成功率并显著降低碰撞率，仅通过预测训练信号实现，无额外推理参数；在Unitree Go2机器人上部署验证了零样本sim-to-real转移，无需微调即可适应室内/室外环境。

Conclusion: 该轻量级预测监督方法有效弥补了反应式策略的不足，为足式机器人在复杂动态环境中提供了高效、实用的导航解决方案，证明了预测训练信号对提升泛化能力的关键作用。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning navigation policies for legged robots select actions reactively from current observations and short-term memory, with limited capacity to anticipate how moving obstacles will evolve in the near future. In dynamic environments, this reactivity causes the robot to respond too late because collision risk depends on short-horizon scene structure rather than on current obstacle positions alone. Lightweight predictive supervision applied to the policy's recurrent state during training can encode anticipatory obstacle dynamics without modifying the inference-time controller. We augment a reactive LSTM-SRU navigation backbone with an auxiliary JEPA-style predictor and SIGReg regularization: during training, the predictor supervises the deterministic hidden state to anticipate its own next state; at inference, it is fully discarded, incurring zero additional computational cost. On simulated and real-world navigation benchmarks with dynamic obstacles, our method substantially improves navigation success while reducing collision rates through the predictive training signal alone, without additional inference-time parameters. Real-robot deployment on a Unitree Go2 demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer: the controller navigates cluttered indoor and dynamic outdoor environments without fine-tuning, with evasive behavior consistent with the collision reduction observed in simulation.

</details>


### [180] [Stability and Comfort in Mobile Robot-Pedestrian Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17604)
*Alireza Jafari,Hong-Son Nguyen,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出针对非全向移动机器人的社会感知导航算法，采用Social Force Model (SFM) 和 TSFM 框架，通过模拟校准和实验验证，证明系统稳定性并显著提升行人舒适度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动机器人导航研究缺乏对行人主观安全的经验验证，经典算法未区分行人与动态障碍物，且忽略主观人类因素；多数研究聚焦全向机器人，但实际应用急需适用于非全向移动机器人（NMR）的社会感知解决方案。

Method: 设计基于 Social Force Model (SFM) 和投影碰撞时间社会力模型（TSFM）的框架，形式化 NMR-行人及 NMR-障碍物交互机制，在假设行人有界非被动条件下证明系统稳定性；通过模拟最大化舒适度与速度混合成本函数校准模型。

Result: 行人-机器人交互实验对比 SFM/TSFM 与远程操控基线，采集行人舒适度反馈；统计分析显示所提算法在舒适度指标上优于前人方法，且基准测试证实其在人群导航中的性能优势。

Conclusion: 模型验证稳定，在 NMR 人群导航场景中有效改善行人主观舒适度，为公共空间机器人应用提供可靠技术方案。

Abstract: Mobile robots in public spaces must ensure pedestrians' comfort, and yet empirical studies of walkers' subjective safety are rare. Many classical navigation algorithms do not distinguish the walkers from dynamic obstacles and do not explicitly model subjective human factors. Moreover, most studies focus on holonomic mobile robots, whereas applications demand Nonholonomic Mobile Robots (NMR). This paper develops socially aware algorithms for NMRs, proves the stability, verifies the performance experimentally, and statistically analyzes the reported comfort. We design a framework for NMRs using Social Force Model (SFM) and the projected Time-to-collision Social Force Model (TSFM). We formalize the NMR-pedestrians' and NMR-obstacles' interactions and prove the system's stability, assuming boundedly nonpassive pedestrians. Simulations calibrate the models by maximizing a hybrid cost function of comfort and speed. Pedestrian-robot interaction experiments compare SFM and TSFM to two remote-controlled baselines and collect walkers' reported comfort. Statistical tools analyze survey results collected during the experiments. Benchmarking the algorithms against previous studies highlights the proposed methods' advantage with respect to the studied metrics. Overall, the models are stable and improve pedestrian comfort when an NMR navigates through a pedestrian crowd.

</details>


### [181] [Configuration-Induced Passive Self-Rotation for Perception-Enhanced Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17646)
*Xurui Liu,Miao Wang,Tianyu He,Linrui Yang,Xiaobin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于配置诱导的被动自旋转三旋翼机，通过调节后臂参数平衡视场覆盖率与飞行性能，并结合分层自主框架实现复杂环境中的高效自主飞行，实验验证了其感知增强效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主飞行在狭窄杂乱环境中受限于机载传感器视场范围，被动自旋转虽可扩大感知覆盖，但需在扫视刷新率与飞行性能间权衡。

Method: 1. 利用后臂配置参数调控被动自旋转工作点，实现视场刷新率与飞行性能的动态平衡；2. 开发集成规划与控制的层次化自主框架，支持持续自旋转下的敏捷飞行；3. 在巡检任务中采用引导点重规划策略提升覆盖效率。

Result: 通过高速轨迹跟踪、抗扰测试及复杂环境导航实验，验证了系统在保持飞行稳定性的同时显著提升感知能力，尤其在动态环境中表现优异。

Conclusion: 该方案有效突破了传统传感器视场限制，为狭窄环境下的感知增强型自主飞行提供了轻量化、高效率的技术路径，具有重要的工程应用价值。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in confined and cluttered environments is fundamentally limited by the restricted field of view (FoV) of onboard sensors. Passive self-rotation expands sensing coverage without additional sensors but introduces a tradeoff between swept-FoV refresh rate and flight performance. This letter presents a configuration-induced passively self-rotating tricopter for perception-enhanced autonomous flight. Firstly, the rear-arm configuration parameter is exploited to regulate the passive self-rotation operating point, providing an airframe-level mechanism for balancing swept-FoV refresh rate and flight performance. Secondly, a hierarchical autonomy framework integrating planning and control is developed to enable agile and robust autonomous flight under continuous passive self-rotation. For waypoint-based inspection, guide-point replanning is further used to improve task-level coverage. Extensive real-world experiments, including high-speed trajectory tracking, disturbance-rejection tests, and autonomous navigation in representative cluttered environments, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach for perception-enhanced autonomous flight.

</details>


### [182] [HCPG-Flow:Hierarchical Contact-Progress Guidance for Flow-Policy Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17651)
*Guanghu Xie,Mingxu Li,Shuo Zhang,Yonglong Zhang,Yifan Yang,Yang Liu,Zongwu Xie,Baoshi Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HCPG-Flow通过改进SAC-Flow的动作选择机制，在仿真与物理机器人操控任务中显著提升成功率并减少执行步骤，解决了传统方法在弱表示数据中的效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于批评家排序的方法在处理多模态动作分布时，依赖候选动作的价值估计，而这些估计在回放数据中可能表现不佳，导致数据收集效率低下。因此需要一种更鲁棒的动作选择机制。

Method: 提出HCPG-Flow分析性运行时选择器，采用四层设计：1) 接触后从末端执行器接近切换至任务进度评估；2) 基于任务相关距离一阶减少量对候选动作进行评分；3) 标准化候选集得分；4) 执行温度控制的动作嵌入策略，同时保留SAC-Flow的原始策略与价值目标。

Result: 在10个仿真任务中，HCPG-Flow相较SAC-Flow平均成功率提升9.5个百分点（Maniskill基准测试）；4项物理任务成功完成步数减少17.4%，同时保持高成功率。

Conclusion: HCPG-Flow通过引入分层、物体中心驱动的接触进度指导机制，有效解决了传统基于批评家排序方法在弱表示回放数据中的局限性，显著提升了机器人操控任务的成功率和执行效率，验证了其在仿真与真实场景中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Flow policies can represent multimodal action distributions for robot manipulation, yet a robot must execute one action at each control step. When several proposals are sampled, critic-based ranking makes data collection depend on value estimates over candidate actions that may be weakly represented in replay. We introduce HCPG-Flow, an analytic rollout-time selector that augments SAC-Flow with hierarchical, object-centric contact-progress guidance while preserving its actor and critic objectives. HCPG switches from end-effector approach to task progress after contact, scores each proposal by the first-order reduction of a task-relevant distance, standardizes scores within the candidate set, and executes a temperature-controlled action embedding. Across ten simulated tasks, HCPG improves mean success over SAC-Flow on both benchmarks, including a 9.5 percentage-point gain on Maniskill. Four physical tasks further show high success with a 17.4% reduction in successful completion steps.Project page: https://hitxraz.github.io/HCPG-Flow/

</details>


### [183] [SLAM in Low-Light Environments: Project Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17699)
*Oleh Basystyi,Anna Stasyshyn,Oleksandr Kosovan,Yaroslav Prytula*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文评估六种SLAM系统在低光照条件下的性能，Kimera-VIO是唯一完成所有序列的系统但存在累积误差，RGB-only SLAM需惯性融合和全局优化才能稳定跟踪，未来改进依赖低光专用学习前端或互补传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照条件下，对比度降低、传感器噪声和运动模糊会劣化特征提取与匹配，而添加LiDAR、深度或热成像传感器会增加成本、功耗和集成复杂度；现有基准多聚焦良好光照场景，故需探索标准RGB相机在黑暗环境中的SLAM潜力。

Method: 对六种范式系统（特征-based、direct、filter-based、learning-based）——ORB-SLAM3、DSO、Kimera-VIO、OpenVINS、DPVO和DPV-SLAM——在五个LaMARia序列（不同难度和光照）上进行基准测试，报告绝对/相对姿态误差及控制点召回率。

Result: Kimera-VIO唯一完成全部序列，具有最低相对姿态误差但绝对误差因缺闭环而稳定增长；DPVO和DPV-SLAM永不丢失跟踪但低光下绝对误差约100米；经典单目管道（ORB-SLAM3、DSO）和filter-based OpenVINS在多数困难/低光序列上完全失败或发散。

Conclusion: RGB-only SLAM仅在同时具备惯性融合和全局优化时维持稳定低光跟踪，剩余差距的缩小需低光特异性学习前端或回归互补传感方案。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is one of the fundamental problems in robotics, as it enables autonomous operations in real-world scenarios. Under low illumination, reduced contrast, sensor noise, and motion blur degrade both feature extraction and feature matching, while compensating with LiDAR, depth, or thermal sensors raises cost, power draw, and integration complexity. Existing benchmarks remain dominated by well-lit indoor or daylight sequences, leaving open how far SLAM with standard RGB cameras can be pushed in the dark. We benchmark six systems spanning the feature-based, direct, filter-based, and learning-based paradigms - ORB-SLAM3, DSO, Kimera-VIO, OpenVINS, DPVO, and DPV-SLAM - on five LaMARia sequences of varying difficulty and illumination, reporting absolute and relative pose error alongside control-point recall. Kimera-VIO is the only system to track all five sequences to completion, combining the lowest relative pose error with steadily growing absolute error due to the absence of loop closure; DPVO and DPV-SLAM never lose tracking but incur absolute errors of roughly 100 m under low light; and the classical monocular pipelines (ORB-SLAM3, DSO) together with the filter-based OpenVINS fail outright or diverge on most of the harder and low-light sequences. The results suggest that RGB-only SLAM maintains stable low-light tracking only when both inertial fusion and global optimization are present. Closing the remaining gap will likely require low-light-specific learned front-ends or a return to complementary sensing.

</details>


### [184] [Lifelong Multi-Subsystem Pickup and Delivery with Buffer-Limited Handover Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17724)
*Chuanlong Zang,Isabelle Barz,Anna Mannucci,Philipp Schillinger,Florian Lier,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Coordinating payload transfers between subsystems is a critical challenge in lifelong Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD). We study systems where agents are confined to separate regions and must exchange payloads through shared handover stations. These stations, equipped with single docks and finite buffers, are inherently vulnerable to blocking and starvation. We formalize this problem as Multi-Subsystem MAPD with Buffer-limited Handover Stations (MS-MAPD-BHS). We then propose Handover-Aware Reservation and Routing (HARR), an online controller that couples per-subsystem planners. HARR uses a shared dock reservation calendar and a deterministic rolling-horizon projection of buffer occupancy to coordinate actions. A candidate route is accepted only if its dock interval is free and the resulting buffer occupancy projection remains within capacity. Under perfect execution, these checks ensure collision-free dock use and buffer-safe committed operations within the reservation horizon. In simulation, HARR achieves up to 77% higher throughput and 92% lower backlog than a fixed-dock ablation at moderate load, while also reducing planning time relative to a coupled station-aware Token Passing baseline. These results show that explicit interface coordination substantially improves stability in modular multi-subsystem transport.

</details>


### [185] [Seg2Grasp: A Robust Modular Suction Grasping in Bin Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17757)
*Hye-Jung Yoon,Juno Kim,Yesol Park,Jun-Ki Lee,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Seg2Grasp通过分割、抓取、分类三步骤，显著提升复杂环境下的机器人BIN抓取成功率与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端学习BIN抓取方法在处理不熟悉或复杂物体时表现不佳，亟需更鲁棒的解决方案以应对动态杂乱环境。

Method: Seg2Grasp采用三阶段模块化流程：Transformer-based分割生成类别无关对象掩码、表面法线结合掩码确定最优吸盘点、微调open-vocabulary Mask-CLIP实现精准物体分类。

Result: 实际机器人实验验证，Seg2Grasp在抓取成功率和环境适应性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Seg2Grasp成为工业自动化BIN抓取的高效工具，有效克服传统方法局限。

Abstract: Current bin picking methods that rely heavily on end-to-end learning often falter when confronted with unfamiliar or complex objects in unstructured environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Seg2Grasp, a modular pipeline designed for robust suction grasping in dynamic and cluttered bin scenarios. Seg2Grasp is built on a three-step process: Segmentation, Grasping, and Classification. The Segmentation module employs a Transformer-based model to generate class-agnostic object masks from RGB-D images, ensuring accurate detection across various conditions. The Grasping module uses surface normals and mask proposals to determine the optimal suction points, enhancing grasp success. Finally, the Classification module leverages fine-tuned open-vocabulary Mask-CLIP for precise object identification, enabling versatile handling of diverse objects. Real-world robotic experiments demonstrate that Seg2Grasp outperforms existing methods in success rates and adaptability, establishing it as a powerful tool for automated bin picking in industrial settings.

</details>


### [186] [VLN-AVP: Zero-Shot Vision-Language Navigation with Hybrid Long-Short-Term Memory for Autonomous Valet Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17767)
*Yijian Li,Xiangru Mu,Changze Li,Hantian Shi,Jiyuan Cai,Jia Cai,Xiaoxue Liu,Yajing Sun,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLN-AVP是一种零样本AVP导航框架，整合BEV感知与VLM智能，消除预建地图依赖，基于新数据集实现高效停车导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVP方法严重依赖预建地图，限制了在未知环境和开放词汇目标的扩展性；受VLM在VLN任务中应用启发，旨在实现无需地图、支持自然语言指令的通用导航。

Method: 提出VLN-AVP框架，融合BEV模型精准空间感知与VLMs语义理解能力，设计混合记忆系统：短期感知记忆跟踪语义视觉线索克服VLM单帧推理局限，长期拓扑记忆存储历史经验促进稳定策略学习。

Result: 仿真实验中成功率较VLN方法提升超25%、较其他自动驾驶方法提升超15%，实车测试达领域领先成功率；创建VLN-AVP数据集（含10高保真车库场景、1000+导航样本），为最大规模地下停车VLN基准。

Conclusion: VLN-AVP有效解决了AVP的地图依赖问题，实现零样本开放环境导航，通过数据集和实验验证了实用性和可扩展性，为AVP技术发展提供新思路。

Abstract: Existing methods in Autonomous Valet Parking (AVP) typically rely on pre-built maps, which severely restricts their scalability to unseen environments and open-vocabulary targets. Inspired by the application of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) tasks, we propose VLN-AVP, a zero-shot navigation framework for AVP tasks. By combining the precise spatial perception of a Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) model with the general intelligence of VLMs, our framework 1) eliminates the dependency on pre-built maps, 2) interprets semantic environmental contexts in parking scenarios, and 3) enables intuitive navigation following natural language instructions. Specifically, we introduce a hybrid memory system: a short-term perception memory tracks semantic visual cues to address the limitations of VLM's single-frame reasoning in existing methods, while a long-term topological memory facilitates stable policy learning from past experiences. To bridge the gap in existing benchmarks, we also present the VLN-AVP dataset and benchmark. Featuring 10 high-fidelity parking scenes and over 1,000 navigation episodes, it has the largest number of garage scenes to date and is the first VLN benchmark for underground parking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that in simulation, our method achieves an over 25% improvement in success rate compared to VLN methods and an over 15% improvement compared to other autonomous driving methods. Furthermore, it attains a leading success rate in real-world vehicle experiments, proving its practical feasibility.

</details>
